# FILE: plot_twist/test_scenery.py
================================================================================

"""Tests for the scenery: floors and walls with details, the depth of the walls, the shading of furniture."""

import pygame
import pytest

import scenery
import sprites
import story


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def display():
    pygame.display.set_mode((1, 1))


def colors(surface, area=None):
    area = area or surface.get_rect()
    return [tuple(surface.get_at((x, y)))[:3] for x in range(area.left, area.right) for y in range(area.top, area.bottom)]


def tile(surface, col, row):
    return colors(surface, pygame.Rect(col * 16, row * 16, 16, 16))


def build(room, columns=10, rows=8, key=None):
    info = story.ROOMS[room]
    wall = sprites.image("wall_" + room) or sprites.image("wall_" + room + "_0")
    return scenery.background(key or ("test", room, columns, rows), columns, rows, sprites.image("floor_" + room), wall,
                              info["floor"], info["outdoor"], scenery.seed_of(room), info.get("damp", False))


# --- Numbers from coordinates ------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_the_hash_depends_only_on_the_coordinates_and_the_seed():
    values = [scenery.hash01(x, y, 3) for x in range(40) for y in range(40)]
    assert all(0 <= value < 1 for value in values)
    assert len(set(values)) > 1500  # spread out, not a few values
    assert 0.4 < sum(values) / len(values) < 0.6
    assert scenery.hash01(5, 9, 3) == scenery.hash01(5, 9, 3) != scenery.hash01(9, 5, 3)
    assert scenery.hash01(5, 9, 3) != scenery.hash01(5, 9, 4)
    assert scenery.seed_of("cabin") == scenery.seed_of("cabin") != scenery.seed_of("galley")


def test_value_noise_is_smooth():
    row = [scenery.value_noise(x, 10, 20, 7) for x in range(200)]
    assert all(0 <= value <= 1 for value in row)
    assert max(abs(one - two) for one, two in zip(row, row[1:])) < 0.12  # no jumps from a pixel to the next
    assert max(row) - min(row) > 0.4  # but it does change across the room


# --- Floors -----------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_every_room_has_a_floor_material():
    assert {info["floor"] for info in story.ROOMS.values()} <= {"wood", "stone", "tiles", "earth"}
    assert story.ROOMS["cabin"]["floor"] == "wood" and story.ROOMS["hold"]["floor"] == "stone"
    assert story.ROOMS["mine"]["floor"] == "earth" and story.ROOMS["infirmary"]["floor"] == "tiles"
    assert {room for room, info in story.ROOMS.items() if info.get("damp")} == {"hold", "corridor", "corridor_end", "mine"}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("room", ["cabin", "hold", "mine", "infirmary"])
def test_no_two_tiles_of_a_floor_are_the_same(room):
    picture = build(room)
    tiles = [tuple(tile(picture, col, row)) for col in range(1, 9) for row in range(1, 7)]
    assert len(set(tiles)) >= len(tiles) * (0.95 if room != "infirmary" else 0.2)  # polished floors: a few hairlines


def test_the_same_room_is_always_drawn_the_same():
    one = build("hold", key=("same", 1))
    two = build("hold", key=("same", 2))
    assert colors(one) == colors(two)
    assert build("hold", key=("same", 1)) is one  # and it is built only once


def test_wooden_planks_do_not_line_up_and_have_nails_and_grain():
    picture = build("cabin", columns=16, rows=8, key=("planks",))
    plank = scenery.colors_of(sprites.image("floor_cabin"))[0]
    seam = min(scenery.colors_of(sprites.image("floor_cabin"))[:3], key=scenery.brightness)
    ends = []
    for strip in range(4, 4 * 24, 4):
        y = 16 + strip
        if y + 3 > 16 * 7:
            break
        ends.append(tuple(x for x in range(17, 16 * 15 - 1) if tuple(picture.get_at((x, y)))[:3] == seam
                          and tuple(picture.get_at((x, y + 2)))[:3] == seam))
        assert tuple(picture.get_at((40, y - 1)))[:3] == seam  # the gap between two rows of planks
    assert len(set(ends)) == len(ends)  # no two rows of planks end in the same places
    gaps = [two - one for row in ends for one, two in zip(row, row[1:])]
    assert min(gaps) >= 18 and max(gaps) > 32  # planks of different lengths
    nails = sum(1 for color in colors(picture) if scenery.brightness(color) > scenery.brightness(plank) + 15)
    assert nails > 20
    shades = set(colors(picture, pygame.Rect(16, 16, 16 * 14, 16 * 6)))
    assert len(shades) >= 5  # plank, gap, grain, a lighter worn edge, nails


def test_stone_floors_have_cracks_and_damp_stains():
    dry = build("hold", key=("dry",))
    info = story.ROOMS["hold"]
    floor = sprites.image("floor_hold")
    plain = pygame.Surface((160, 128))
    for col in range(10):
        for row in range(8):
            plain.blit(floor, (col * 16, row * 16))
    inside = pygame.Rect(16, 16, 128, 96)
    changed = sum(1 for one, two in zip(colors(dry, inside), colors(plain, inside)) if one != two)
    assert changed > 1000  # slabs darker and lighter, cracks, damp
    assert info["damp"]
    bluer = sum(1 for one, two in zip(colors(dry, inside), colors(plain, inside))
                if one != two and one[2] - one[0] > two[2] - two[0])
    assert bluer > 100  # the damp is darker and bluer


def test_damp_comes_in_hard_bands_dithered_where_it_starts():
    surface = pygame.Surface((200, 120))
    surface.fill((120, 120, 120))
    scenery.damp(surface, surface.get_rect(), 5)
    seen = set(colors(surface))
    assert len(seen) == 2 and (120, 120, 120) in seen  # dry, or wet: one hard band of colour, no fade
    wet = (seen - {(120, 120, 120)}).pop()
    assert sum(wet) < 360 and wet[2] > wet[0]  # darker, and bluer
    dithered = solid = 0
    for x in range(0, 200, 2):
        for y in range(0, 120, 2):
            block = [tuple(surface.get_at((x + dx, y + dy)))[:3] for dx in (0, 1) for dy in (0, 1)]
            wet = sum(1 for color in block if color != (120, 120, 120))
            dithered += wet == 2
            solid += wet == 4
    assert dithered > 20 and solid > 5


# --- Walls ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_the_back_wall_has_a_light_top_a_face_and_a_dark_foot():
    picture = build("galley", key=("wall",))

    def row(y):  # how bright a row of the back wall is, on average
        return sum(scenery.brightness(picture.get_at((x, y))) for x in range(16, 16 * 9)) / (16 * 8)
    top, under_cap = row(0), row(scenery.CAP - 1)
    face = sum(row(y) for y in range(scenery.CAP, 13)) / (13 - scenery.CAP)
    foot = row(15)
    assert top > face > foot  # lit on top, darker down to the floor
    assert under_cap < face  # a dark line where the top meets the face
    assert row(1) > row(scenery.CAP + 1)


def test_the_walls_cast_a_short_shadow_on_the_floor():
    picture = build("galley", key=("shadow",))

    def row(y):
        return sum(scenery.brightness(picture.get_at((x, y))) for x in range(20, 140)) / 120

    def column(x):
        return sum(scenery.brightness(picture.get_at((x, y))) for y in range(20, 100)) / 80
    open_floor = sum(row(y) for y in range(28, 60)) / 32
    assert row(16) < open_floor * 0.75 and row(17) < open_floor * 0.75  # right under the back wall: solid shade
    assert row(16 + scenery.WALL_SHADOW[0] - 1) < open_floor  # dithered further out
    assert open_floor * 0.9 < row(16 + scenery.WALL_SHADOW[0] + 2)  # and then the floor as it is
    open_column = sum(column(x) for x in range(30, 60)) / 30
    assert column(16) < open_column * 0.8  # beside the left wall too


def test_the_side_walls_show_their_top_with_a_light_edge_on_the_room_side():
    picture = build("galley", key=("sides",))
    wall = sprites.image("wall_galley")
    assert scenery.brightness(picture.get_at((15, 40))) > scenery.brightness(picture.get_at((8, 40)))  # left wall
    assert scenery.brightness(picture.get_at((9 * 16, 40))) > scenery.brightness(picture.get_at((9 * 16 + 8, 40)))
    plain = scenery.brightness(wall.get_at((8, 8)))
    assert scenery.brightness(picture.get_at((8, 16 * 3 + 8))) > plain  # the top catches the light


def test_an_outdoor_room_keeps_its_sea_and_gets_no_walls():
    picture = build("deck", key=("deck",))
    sea = sprites.image("wall_deck_0")
    assert colors(picture, pygame.Rect(0, 0, 16, 16)) == colors(sea)


# --- Furniture --------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_furniture_has_no_plain_black_outline_but_a_dark_shade_of_its_colour():
    for name in ("decor_barrel", "decor_crate", "decor_bed", "decor_stove", "decor_table"):
        plain = sprites.image(name)
        shaded = scenery.shaded(name, plain)
        assert shaded.get_size() == plain.get_size()
        outline = [(x, y) for x in range(16) for y in range(16) if tuple(plain.get_at((x, y)))[:3] == scenery.OUTLINE
                   and plain.get_at((x, y))[3]]
        assert outline
        for x, y in outline:
            assert tuple(shaded.get_at((x, y)))[:3] != scenery.OUTLINE, (name, x, y)
            assert scenery.brightness(shaded.get_at((x, y))) < 90, (name, x, y)  # still dark: a clean edge
        alpha = [plain.get_at((x, y))[3] == shaded.get_at((x, y))[3] for x in range(16) for y in range(16)]
        assert all(alpha)  # the same shape


def test_shading_is_lighter_on_the_top_left_and_darker_on_the_bottom_right():
    plain = sprites.image("decor_crate")
    shaded = scenery.shaded("decor_crate", plain)
    lighter = darker = 0
    for x in range(16):
        for y in range(16):
            one, two = plain.get_at((x, y)), shaded.get_at((x, y))
            if not one[3] or tuple(one)[:3] == scenery.OUTLINE:
                continue
            if scenery.brightness(two) > scenery.brightness(one):
                lighter += 1
                assert x < 13 and y < 13
            elif scenery.brightness(two) < scenery.brightness(one):
                darker += 1
    assert lighter > 10 and darker > 10
    assert scenery.shaded("decor_crate", plain) is shaded  # made once


def test_the_hue_shifts_cool_in_the_shade_and_warm_in_the_light():
    red = (214, 64, 52)
    dark, light = scenery.darker(red, 0.4), scenery.lighter(red, 0.2)
    assert scenery.brightness(dark) < scenery.brightness(red) < scenery.brightness(light)
    assert dark[2] / max(dark[0], 1) > red[2] / red[0]  # bluer in the shade
    assert light[2] / light[0] < (red[2] + (255 - red[2]) * 0.2) / (red[0] + (255 - red[0]) * 0.2)  # warmer in the light


def test_the_ground_shadow_is_an_oval_solid_in_the_middle_and_dithered_around():
    shadow = scenery.ground_shadow(14, 5)
    assert shadow.get_size() == (14, 5)
    assert shadow.get_at((7, 2))[3] == scenery.SHADOW_ALPHA  # solid in the middle
    assert shadow.get_at((0, 0))[3] == 0 and shadow.get_at((13, 4))[3] == 0  # round: no corners
    edge = [shadow.get_at((x, 2))[3] for x in range(0, 3)]
    assert 0 in edge and scenery.SHADOW_ALPHA in edge  # dithered at its ends
    assert set(shadow.get_at((x, y))[3] for x in range(14) for y in range(5)) == {0, scenery.SHADOW_ALPHA}
    assert scenery.ground_shadow(14, 5) is shadow


================================================================================
# FILE: plot_twist/test_claw.py
================================================================================

"""Tests for the giant claw: where its joints are at each moment of the attack, and how it is painted."""

import math

import pygame
import pytest

import claw

SIZE = (256, 224)
REACH, ATTACK, IMPACT, RETREAT = 10, 15, 27, 12
TONES = {claw.OUTLINE, claw.SHADOW, claw.BASE, claw.LIGHT, claw.PLATE, claw.TOOTH}


def pose(age):
    return claw.pose(age, SIZE, REACH, ATTACK, IMPACT, RETREAT)


def gap(a, b):
    return math.hypot(a[0] - b[0], a[1] - b[1])


def angle(a, b):
    return math.atan2(b[1] - a[1], b[0] - a[0])


def turn(first, second):
    """The angle between two directions, in degrees, from 0 to 180."""
    return abs(math.degrees(math.atan2(math.sin(first - second), math.cos(first - second))))


def painted(age):
    pygame.display.set_mode((1, 1))
    return claw.layer(SIZE, pose(age))


def pixels(picture):
    return [(x, y, tuple(picture.get_at((x, y)))) for x in range(SIZE[0]) for y in range(SIZE[1])]


# --- The pose ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_the_claw_comes_in_from_outside_and_closes_on_the_middle_of_the_screen():
    screen = pygame.Rect(0, 0, *SIZE)
    middle = (SIZE[0] / 2, SIZE[1] / 2)
    assert not screen.collidepoint(pose(0)["tip"])
    assert all(color[3] == 0 for x, y, color in pixels(painted(0)))  # nothing of it in sight before it strikes
    gaps = [gap(pose(age)["tip"], middle) for age in range(ATTACK)]
    assert gaps == sorted(gaps, reverse=True) and gaps[0] > 150  # closer and closer
    assert gap(pose(REACH)["tip"], middle) < 0.01 and pose(REACH)["opening"] == claw.JAW_OPEN  # there, still open
    openings = [pose(age)["opening"] for age in range(REACH, ATTACK)]
    assert openings == sorted(openings, reverse=True) and openings[-1] == 0.0  # the jaws shut
    assert gap(pose(ATTACK - 1)["tip"], middle) < 0.01


def test_the_limb_has_three_segments_that_keep_their_length_and_stand_at_an_angle():
    for age in range(0, IMPACT + RETREAT + 2):
        limb = pose(age)
        assert gap(limb["shoulder"], limb["elbow"]) == pytest.approx(claw.UPPER_ARM, abs=0.01), age
        assert gap(limb["elbow"], limb["wrist"]) == pytest.approx(claw.FOREARM, abs=0.01), age
        assert gap(limb["wrist"], limb["hinge"]) == pytest.approx(claw.HAND, abs=0.01), age
    limb = pose(ATTACK - 1)
    upper = angle(limb["shoulder"], limb["elbow"])
    fore = angle(limb["elbow"], limb["wrist"])
    hand = angle(limb["wrist"], limb["hinge"])
    assert turn(upper, fore) > 15 and turn(fore, hand) > 8 and turn(upper, hand) > 15  # none in line with another
    screen = pygame.Rect(0, 0, *SIZE)
    for name in ("elbow", "wrist", "hinge", "tip"):
        assert screen.collidepoint(limb[name]), name  # all three segments are on screen at the hit
    assert not screen.collidepoint(limb["shoulder"])  # the limb comes from beyond the corner
    assert limb["shoulder"][0] > SIZE[0] and limb["shoulder"][1] < 0


def test_the_joints_flex_during_the_lunge_and_as_the_jaws_shut():
    def elbow_bend(age):
        limb = pose(age)
        return turn(angle(limb["shoulder"], limb["elbow"]), angle(limb["elbow"], limb["wrist"]))

    def wrist_bend(age):
        limb = pose(age)
        return turn(angle(limb["elbow"], limb["wrist"]), limb["heading"])
    assert elbow_bend(4) > elbow_bend(REACH) + 20   # folded at first, the arm stretches out as it strikes
    assert abs(elbow_bend(REACH) - elbow_bend(ATTACK - 1)) > 3   # and gives a little as the jaws shut
    assert abs(wrist_bend(REACH) - wrist_bend(ATTACK - 1)) > 2   # the wrist gives too
    assert turn(pose(REACH)["heading"], pose(ATTACK - 1)["heading"]) > 12  # as the hand snaps forward on the grip
    headings = [pose(age)["heading"] for age in range(ATTACK)]
    assert headings[0] == pytest.approx(claw.AIM + claw.WRIST_COCKED) and headings[-1] == pytest.approx(claw.AIM + claw.WRIST_STRUCK)


def test_at_the_hit_the_arm_kicks_back_along_its_axis_then_shivers_and_holds():
    rest = pose(ATTACK - 1)["tip"]
    back = (-math.cos(claw.AIM), -math.sin(claw.AIM))  # away from the screen, along the hand
    moves = []
    for age in range(ATTACK, IMPACT):
        tip = pose(age)["tip"]
        along = (tip[0] - rest[0]) * back[0] + (tip[1] - rest[1]) * back[1]
        across = (tip[0] - rest[0]) * back[1] - (tip[1] - rest[1]) * back[0]
        assert abs(across) < 0.01, age  # only along the arm
        moves.append(round(along))
    assert moves[0] in (2, 3) and moves[1] in (2, 3)             # the kick: two or three pixels back
    assert set(moves[3:7]) <= {-1, 0, 1} and len(set(moves[3:7])) >= 2  # then a shiver of a pixel
    assert moves[len(claw.RECOIL):] == [0] * (IMPACT - ATTACK - len(claw.RECOIL))  # and it holds still
    assert pose(ATTACK + 1)["opening"] == 0.0


def test_after_the_screen_breaks_the_claw_pulls_back_out():
    gaps = [gap(pose(age)["tip"], pose(ATTACK - 1)["tip"]) for age in range(IMPACT, IMPACT + RETREAT + 1)]
    assert gaps == sorted(gaps)
    assert pose(IMPACT + RETREAT)["tip"] == pytest.approx(pose(0)["tip"])
    assert not pygame.Rect(0, 0, *SIZE).collidepoint(pose(IMPACT + RETREAT + 5)["tip"])


def test_the_shadow_is_far_while_the_claw_is_high_and_touches_it_at_the_hit():
    heights = [pose(age)["shadow"] for age in range(ATTACK)]
    assert heights[0] == 1.0 and heights == sorted(heights, reverse=True) and len(set(heights)) == ATTACK
    assert heights[-1] == 0.0  # the frame before the snap: nothing between the claw and the screen
    assert all(pose(age)["shadow"] == 0.0 for age in range(ATTACK, IMPACT + RETREAT))


def test_the_two_jaws_meet_at_their_tips_when_shut_and_gape_when_open():
    for heading in (0.0, claw.AIM, 2.0):
        hinge = (100.0, 100.0)
        shut = [claw.jaw_line(hinge, heading, side, 0.0) for side in (1, -1)]
        assert gap(shut[0][-1], shut[1][-1]) < 5  # tip to tip
        reach = claw.jaw_reach()
        meet = (hinge[0] + reach * math.cos(heading), hinge[1] + reach * math.sin(heading))
        assert gap(shut[0][-1], meet) < 3 and gap(shut[1][-1], meet) < 3
        wide = [claw.jaw_line(hinge, heading, side, claw.JAW_OPEN) for side in (1, -1)]
        assert gap(wide[0][-1], wide[1][-1]) > 40
        middle = len(shut[0]) // 2
        assert gap(shut[0][middle], shut[1][middle]) > gap(shut[0][-1], shut[1][-1]) + 10  # curved: a grip, not a beak
        assert len(shut[0]) == claw.JAW_STEPS + 1 == len(claw.JAW_WIDTHS)


def test_the_elbow_is_where_the_two_bones_meet_on_the_outside_of_the_arm():
    shoulder = (272.0, -42.0)
    for wrist in ((190.0, 62.0), (230.0, 20.0), (265.0, -30.0)):
        elbow = claw.elbow_between(shoulder, wrist)
        assert gap(shoulder, elbow) == pytest.approx(claw.UPPER_ARM, abs=0.01)
        if gap(shoulder, wrist) > abs(claw.UPPER_ARM - claw.FOREARM):
            assert gap(elbow, wrist) == pytest.approx(claw.FOREARM, abs=0.01)
        straight = (wrist[0] - shoulder[0], wrist[1] - shoulder[1])
        side = straight[0] * (elbow[1] - shoulder[1]) - straight[1] * (elbow[0] - shoulder[0])
        assert side < 0  # always the same side: the elbow never flips over
    assert claw.elbow_between(shoulder, (1000.0, 1000.0))  # out of reach: no error, the arm is straight


# --- The painting -----------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_the_limb_is_painted_in_its_four_tones_with_hard_pixels():
    for age in (6, REACH, ATTACK - 1, ATTACK + 1):
        picture = painted(age)
        assert picture.get_size() == SIZE  # the small screen: nothing enlarged
        seen = {}
        for x, y, (r, g, b, a) in pixels(picture):
            assert a in (0, 255), (age, x, y)  # no soft edges
            if a:
                assert (r, g, b) in TONES, (age, x, y, (r, g, b))  # no colour in between: nothing blurred
                seen[(r, g, b)] = seen.get((r, g, b), 0) + 1
        for tone in (claw.OUTLINE, claw.SHADOW, claw.BASE, claw.LIGHT):
            assert seen.get(tone, 0) > 150, (age, tone)
        assert seen[claw.BASE] > seen[claw.LIGHT] and seen[claw.BASE] > seen[claw.OUTLINE]  # red, mostly
        assert seen.get(claw.PLATE, 0) > 100  # the dark plates of the joints


def test_the_light_comes_from_the_top_left():
    picture = painted(ATTACK - 1)
    lit = [(x, y) for x, y, color in pixels(picture) if color[:3] == claw.LIGHT and color[3]]
    dark = [(x, y) for x, y, color in pixels(picture) if color[:3] == claw.SHADOW and color[3]]
    def middle(points):
        return sum(x + y for x, y in points) / len(points)
    assert middle(lit) < middle(dark) - 6  # the pale edge is up and to the left of the shadow


def test_the_shell_is_not_flat_dither_and_grains():
    picture = painted(ATTACK - 1)
    area = picture.get_rect()

    def color(x, y):
        return tuple(picture.get_at((x, y)))[:3] if area.collidepoint(x, y) and picture.get_at((x, y))[3] else None
    lone = 0
    for x, y, (r, g, b, a) in pixels(picture):
        if a and (r, g, b) in (claw.SHADOW, claw.OUTLINE):
            around = [color(x + dx, y + dy) for dx, dy in ((1, 0), (-1, 0), (0, 1), (0, -1))]
            lone += all(other == claw.BASE for other in around)  # a dark pixel alone in the red
    assert lone >= 25


def test_spikes_stand_on_the_outer_edge_of_the_forearm_and_teeth_inside_the_grip(monkeypatch):
    thorns = []
    real = claw.thorn

    def spy(surface, base, direction, wide, tall, color):
        thorns.append((base, direction, tall, color))
        real(surface, base, direction, wide, tall, color)
    monkeypatch.setattr(claw, "thorn", spy)
    limb = pose(REACH)
    pygame.display.set_mode((1, 1))
    picture = claw.layer(SIZE, limb)
    spikes = [one for one in thorns if one[3] != claw.TOOTH]
    teeth = [one for one in thorns if one[3] == claw.TOOTH]
    assert len(spikes) == claw.SPIKES >= 4 and len(teeth) == 2 * claw.TEETH
    assert len({one[2] for one in spikes}) >= 2  # a saw: not all the same height
    elbow, wrist = limb["elbow"], limb["wrist"]
    bend = (elbow[0] - (limb["shoulder"][0] + wrist[0]) / 2, elbow[1] - (limb["shoulder"][1] + wrist[1]) / 2)
    for base, direction, tall, color in spikes:
        assert direction[0] * bend[0] + direction[1] * bend[1] > 0  # on the outside of the elbow's bend
        assert min(gap(base, elbow), gap(base, wrist)) < claw.FOREARM  # along the forearm
    axis = (math.cos(limb["heading"]), math.sin(limb["heading"]))
    for base, direction, tall, color in teeth:
        off = (base[0] - limb["hinge"][0]) * -axis[1] + (base[1] - limb["hinge"][1]) * axis[0]
        towards = direction[0] * -axis[1] + direction[1] * axis[0]
        assert off * towards < 0  # each tooth points across the grip, at the other jaw
    assert any(tuple(picture.get_at((x, y)))[:3] == claw.TOOTH for x in range(SIZE[0]) for y in range(SIZE[1]))


def test_the_joints_are_dark_plates_one_over_the_other():
    picture = painted(ATTACK - 1)
    limb = pose(ATTACK - 1)
    for name, radius in (("elbow", claw.ELBOW), ("wrist", claw.WRIST), ("hinge", claw.HINGE)):
        x, y = round(limb[name][0]), round(limb[name][1])
        near = {tuple(picture.get_at((x + dx, y + dy)))[:3] for dx in range(-radius, radius + 1)
                for dy in range(-radius, radius + 1) if 0 <= x + dx < SIZE[0] and 0 <= y + dy < SIZE[1]}
        assert {claw.PLATE, claw.OUTLINE, claw.SHADOW} <= near, name  # the plate, its outline, the plate over it


def test_the_shadow_has_the_shape_of_the_limb_in_see_through_black():
    picture = painted(8)
    shadow = claw.shadow_of(picture)
    assert shadow.get_size() == SIZE
    for x in range(0, SIZE[0], 3):
        for y in range(0, SIZE[1], 3):
            r, g, b, a = shadow.get_at((x, y))
            assert (a > 0) == (picture.get_at((x, y))[3] > 0), (x, y)
            if a:
                assert (r, g, b) == (0, 0, 0) and 60 < a < 200  # dark, but the game shows through


def test_the_limb_can_be_painted_pushed_aside_and_off_the_screen():
    pygame.display.set_mode((1, 1))
    still = claw.layer(SIZE, pose(ATTACK - 1))
    pushed = claw.layer(SIZE, pose(ATTACK - 1), offset=(4, -3))
    x, y = round(pose(ATTACK - 1)["wrist"][0]), round(pose(ATTACK - 1)["wrist"][1])
    assert pushed.get_at((x + 4, y - 3)) == still.get_at((x, y))
    for age in range(0, IMPACT + RETREAT + 3):
        claw.layer(SIZE, pose(age))  # every frame of the sequence, even with the limb outside



def colours_of(picture):
    return [picture.get_at((x, y)) for x in range(picture.get_width()) for y in range(picture.get_height())
            if picture.get_at((x, y)).a]


def test_the_two_portraits_are_a_common_claw_and_the_red_one_of_the_end():
    gentle = claw.portrait((100, 86), fierce=False)
    fierce = claw.portrait((100, 86), fierce=True)
    for picture, tones in ((gentle, claw.CORAL), (fierce, claw.RED)):
        colours = colours_of(picture)
        assert len(colours) > 900  # a big claw, not a speck
        used = {tuple(colour)[:3] for colour in colours}
        assert {tones["outline"], tones["base"], tones["shadow"], tones["light"]} <= used  # four tones, shaded
        assert used <= set(tones.values())  # hard pixels: nothing but the shell's own tones
    assert claw.CORAL["base"] != claw.RED["base"]
    assert colours_of(gentle)[0] is not None


def test_the_fierce_claw_has_spikes_and_sharp_teeth_the_gentle_one_round_ones():
    assert claw.FIERCE["spikes"] > 0 and claw.GENTLE["spikes"] == 0
    assert claw.FIERCE["jaw"] > claw.GENTLE["jaw"] and claw.FIERCE["curl"] > claw.GENTLE["curl"]  # longer, hooked
    assert max(claw.GENTLE["hand"]) > max(claw.FIERCE["hand"])  # a round, thick palm
    fierce = claw.portrait((100, 86), fierce=True)
    gentle = claw.portrait((100, 86), fierce=False)
    assert pygame.mask.from_surface(fierce).count() != pygame.mask.from_surface(gentle).count()


def test_a_portrait_opens_and_shuts_its_jaws():
    shut = pygame.mask.from_surface(claw.portrait((100, 86), True, 0.05))
    open_ = pygame.mask.from_surface(claw.portrait((100, 86), True, 0.35))
    assert shut.overlap_area(open_, (0, 0)) < max(shut.count(), open_.count())


def test_the_giant_claw_keeps_its_red_shell():
    assert claw.RED == {"outline": claw.OUTLINE, "shadow": claw.SHADOW, "base": claw.BASE, "light": claw.LIGHT,
                        "plate": claw.PLATE, "tooth": claw.TOOTH}

# FILE: plot_twist/test_graphics.py

"""Tests for the 8-bit graphics (M6): the sprites, the pixel font and what moves on screen.

M6 changed how the game looks, not what it does: the tests in the other files
did not change. These check that every thing on screen has a picture, that the
font can write every text of the game, and that the animations behave.
"""

import pygame
import pytest

import engine
import main
import pixel_font
import sprites
import story


@pytest.fixture
def app():
    """The interface just after launching the game, already playing a new game."""
    app = main.new_app(main.load_fonts())
    main.handle_key(app, pygame.K_RETURN)  # "New game"
    main.handle_key(app, pygame.K_RETURN)  # "1 player"
    main.handle_key(app, pygame.K_RETURN)  # the first character
    while app["dialog"]:
        main.handle_key(app, pygame.K_e)
    return app


def p1(app):
    """The first (and for now only) player of the interface."""
    return app["players"][0]


def play(actions):
    state = engine.new_game()
    for verb, target, item in actions:
        state, outcome = engine.perform(state, {"verb": verb, "target": target, "item": item})
        assert outcome["ok"], outcome
    return state


TILES = ["tile_1", "tile_2", "tile_3", "tile_4", "tile_5", "tile_3", "tile_1", "tile_5", "tile_2", "tile_4", "tile_3"]
TO_THE_CRAB_DOOR = ([("pick_up", "wand", None), ("enter", "deck_to_cabin", None), ("activate", "generator_a", None),
                     ("activate", "generator_c", None), ("pick_up", "key", None), ("enter", "cabin_to_deck", None),
                     ("enter", "deck_to_hold", None)]
                    + [("step_on", tile, None) for tile in TILES]
                    + [("pick_up", "second_key", None), ("enter", "hold_to_infirmary", None),
                       ("pick_up", "green_tincture", None), ("enter", "infirmary_to_corridor", None),
                       ("pick_up", "blue_tincture", None), ("enter", "corridor_to_infirmary", None),
                       ("enter", "infirmary_to_hold", None), ("enter", "hold_to_deck", None),
                       ("enter", "deck_to_cabin", None), ("enter", "cabin_to_galley", None),
                       ("pick_up", "red_tincture", None), ("enter", "galley_to_cabin", None),
                       ("enter", "cabin_to_helm", None), ("pick_up", "gold_tincture", None),
                       ("enter", "helm_to_cabin", None), ("enter", "cabin_to_deck", None),
                       ("enter", "deck_to_hold", None), ("enter", "hold_to_infirmary", None)]
                    + [("use_item", "cauldron", tincture) for tincture in story.POTION_PUZZLES["remedy"]["recipe"]]
                    + [("pick_up", "potion", None), ("use_item", "doctor", "potion"), ("enter", "infirmary_to_hold", None),
                       ("enter", "hold_to_deck", None), ("enter", "deck_to_cabin", None), ("enter", "cabin_to_east_room", None),
                       ("use_item", "anvil", "key"), ("enter", "east_room_to_cabin", None), ("enter", "cabin_to_deck", None)])
CRAB_ENDING = TO_THE_CRAB_DOOR + [("use_item", "deck_to_crab", "fused_key"), ("enter", "deck_to_crab", None),
                                  ("use_item", "crab", "wand")]
ANGEL_ENDING = TO_THE_CRAB_DOOR + [("use_item", "deck_to_angel", "fused_key"), ("enter", "deck_to_angel", None),
                                   ("use_item", "angel", "wand")]


# --- Sprites -------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_every_sprite_is_a_proper_picture():
    """Rows of the same length, letters from the palette, and the sizes the game expects."""
    for name, rows in sprites.SPRITES.items():
        assert len({len(row) for row in rows}) == 1, name
        for row in rows:
            for letter in row:
                assert letter == "." or letter in sprites.PALETTE, (name, letter)
        size = (len(rows[0]), len(rows))
        sharing = [thing for thing, info in story.OBJECTS.items() if info.get("picture") == name]
        if name.startswith("door_") or name == "ship":
            assert size == (2 * main.TILE, main.TILE), name
        elif any(name.startswith(info["sprite"] + "_") for info in story.CHARACTERS.values()):
            assert size == (main.TILE, sprites.FIGURE_HEIGHT), name  # the characters: taller than a tile
        elif name == "decor_crab_rubble":  # what is left of the statue: drawn in its place
            assert size == main.thing_rect("deck_statue").size, name
        elif sharing:  # a shared picture is as big as every piece that uses it
            for thing in sharing:
                assert size == main.thing_rect(thing).size, (name, thing)
        elif name in story.OBJECTS:
            assert size == main.thing_rect(name).size, name  # the big characters fill all their tiles
        else:
            assert size == (main.TILE, main.TILE), name


def test_everything_on_screen_has_a_picture():
    """No rectangles left: rooms, items, puzzles, characters, doors and the player all have sprites."""
    for room in story.ROOMS:
        assert sprites.image("floor_" + room) is not None, room
        assert sprites.image("wall_" + room) is not None or sprites.image("wall_" + room + "_0") is not None, room
    for item in story.ITEMS:
        assert sprites.image(item) is not None, item
    statuses = {"puzzle": ["solved", "unsolved"], "character": ["present"], "switch": ["on", "off"],
                "altar": ["present"], "anvil": ["present"], "barrier": ["up"], "tile": ["lit", "dark", "error"], "decor": ["present"],
                "cauldron": ["present"], "portal": ["present"]}
    for thing, info in story.OBJECTS.items():
        for status in statuses[info["kind"]]:
            if info["kind"] == "barrier":
                assert sprites.image("barrier_0") is not None and sprites.image("barrier_1") is not None
            elif info["kind"] == "tile":
                assert sprites.image("tile_" + status) is not None, status  # main.py writes the number on it
            else:
                picture = main.thing_picture({"id": thing, "kind": info["kind"], "status": status})
                assert picture is not None, (thing, status)
    assert sprites.image("zone") is not None
    for status in ("open", "barred", "locked", "sealed"):
        assert sprites.image("door_" + status) is not None, status
    for character in story.CHARACTERS.values():
        for direction in ("down", "up", "right"):
            for step in range(3):
                assert sprites.image(f"{character['sprite']}_{direction}_{step}") is not None, (character, direction, step)
    assert sprites.image("ship") is not None


def test_door_pictures_turn_to_fit_their_wall():
    for door, info in story.DOORS.items():
        picture = sprites.image("door_locked", turns=main.DOOR_TURNS[info["side"]])
        assert picture.get_size() == main.door_rect(door).size, door


def test_a_missing_picture_gives_none():
    assert sprites.image("no_such_picture") is None


def test_flipped_pictures_are_mirrored_pixel_by_pixel():
    picture = sprites.image("wizard_right_1")
    mirrored = sprites.image("wizard_right_1", flip=True)
    width, height = picture.get_size()
    for x in range(width):
        for y in range(height):
            assert mirrored.get_at((x, y)) == picture.get_at((width - 1 - x, y)), (x, y)


def test_turned_pictures_are_rotated_pixel_by_pixel():
    """A quarter turn counterclockwise: the top row becomes the left column, read from the bottom up."""
    picture = sprites.image("door_locked")
    turned = sprites.image("door_locked", turns=1)
    width, height = picture.get_size()
    assert turned.get_size() == (height, width)
    for x in range(height):
        for y in range(width):
            assert turned.get_at((x, y)) == picture.get_at((width - 1 - y, x)), (x, y)


def test_a_thing_without_a_picture_is_drawn_as_a_rectangle(app, monkeypatch):
    """If a sprite is missing, the game still runs: the thing gets its colored rectangle."""
    without_crab = dict(sprites.SPRITES)
    del without_crab["crab"]
    monkeypatch.setattr(sprites, "SPRITES", without_crab)
    app["state"] = play(CRAB_ENDING[:-1])  # in the crab's room
    app["fade"] = 0  # fully lit
    screen = pygame.Surface((main.WIDTH, main.HEIGHT))
    main.draw(app, screen)
    assert tuple(screen.get_at(main.thing_rect("crab").center))[:3] == main.THING_COLORS["crab"]


# --- The pixel font --------------------------------------------------------------------


def game_texts():
    """Every text the game can show, plus what main.py adds around them."""
    texts = list(story.MESSAGES.values()) + list(story.CLUES.values())
    for room in story.ROOMS.values():
        texts += [room["name"], room["description"]]
    for item in story.ITEMS.values():
        texts += [item["name"], item["description"], item["pickup_text"] or ""]
    for door in story.DOORS.values():
        texts += [door["locked_text"] or "", door["sealed_text"] or "", door["open_text"] or ""]
    for thing in story.OBJECTS.values():
        texts += [thing["name"], thing["look_text"], thing["done_text"] or "", thing.get("fuse_text", "")]
        for reaction in thing["reactions"].values():
            texts.append(reaction["text"])
    for ending in story.ENDINGS.values():
        texts += [ending["title"]] + ending["text"]
    texts.append("> < 0123456789")  # the marks around the chosen menu entry, the ending number
    return texts


def test_the_font_can_write_every_text_of_the_game():
    missing = set()
    for text in game_texts():
        for char in text:
            if char not in pixel_font.GLYPHS and char not in "{}":  # braces only mark where values go
                missing.add(char)
    assert missing == set()


def test_every_character_is_a_proper_picture():
    for char, rows in pixel_font.GLYPHS.items():
        assert len(rows) == pixel_font.HEIGHT, char
        assert len({len(row) for row in rows}) == 1, char
        for row in rows:
            assert set(row) <= {"#", "."}, char


@pytest.mark.parametrize("scale", [1, 2, 3])
def test_the_font_draws_exactly_what_it_measures(scale):
    font = pixel_font.PixelFont(scale)
    for text in ["Ponte", "È chiusa a chiave.", "«Il granchio non mente mai.»", "ij", "E: Raccogli"]:
        assert font.render(text, False, (255, 255, 255)).get_size() == font.size(text), text
    assert font.size("") == (0, pixel_font.HEIGHT * scale)
    assert font.get_linesize() == (pixel_font.HEIGHT + pixel_font.LINE_GAP) * scale
    assert font.size("ii")[0] == (1 + pixel_font.SPACING + 1) * scale


def test_an_unknown_character_is_drawn_as_a_box():
    font = pixel_font.PixelFont(1)
    assert font.size("€") == (len(pixel_font.MISSING[0]), pixel_font.HEIGHT)


# --- Animations ------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_the_player_walks_in_two_steps_and_stands_still(app):
    main.update(app, 1, 0)
    assert p1(app)["moving"]
    steps = set()
    for _ in range(4 * main.WALK_STEP_FRAMES):
        main.update(app, 1, 0)
        steps.add(main.player_picture(app, p1(app)))
    assert steps == {sprites.image("wizard_right_1"), sprites.image("wizard_right_2")}
    main.update(app, 0, 0)
    assert not p1(app)["moving"]
    assert main.player_picture(app, p1(app)) is sprites.image("wizard_right_0")


def test_facing_left_mirrors_the_right_pictures(app):
    main.update(app, -1, 0)
    main.update(app, 0, 0)
    assert main.player_picture(app, p1(app)) is sprites.image("wizard_right_0", flip=True)


def test_a_room_comes_out_of_the_dark_after_a_door(app):
    for _ in range(main.FADE_FRAMES):
        main.update(app, 0, 0)
    assert app["fade"] == 0
    p1(app)["pos"] = main.spawn_in_front_of("deck_to_cabin")
    p1(app)["touching"] = None
    while app["state"]["room"] == "deck":
        main.update(app, 0, -1)
    assert app["fade"] == main.FADE_FRAMES
    for _ in range(main.FADE_FRAMES):
        main.update(app, 0, 0)
    assert app["fade"] == 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize("ending_path", [CRAB_ENDING, ANGEL_ENDING])
def test_the_endings_play_their_animation(app, ending_path):
    """Every frame of the first five seconds of each ending can be drawn, and the story on screen moves on.

    The pictures compared are two seconds apart, when the waves and the ship's
    swaying are back where they started: only the ending's own animation differs.
    """
    app["state"] = play(ending_path)
    main.set_scene(app, "ending")
    app["frame"] = 0
    screen = pygame.Surface((main.WIDTH, main.HEIGHT))
    pictures = {}
    for frame in range(5 * main.FPS):
        main.draw(app, screen)
        if frame in (0, 2 * main.FPS):
            pictures[frame] = pygame.image.tobytes(screen, "RGB")
        main.update(app, 0, 0)
    assert pictures[0] != pictures[2 * main.FPS]




# --- The characters, 16 x 24 ---------------------------------------------------------------


def character_pictures():
    for info in story.CHARACTERS.values():
        for direction in ("down", "up", "right"):
            for step in range(3):
                yield info["sprite"], direction, step, sprites.SPRITES[f"{info['sprite']}_{direction}_{step}"]


def letters(rows, first, last):
    return {letter for row in rows[first:last + 1] for letter in row} - {"."}


def test_every_character_is_16_by_24_with_its_feet_on_the_bottom_row():
    assert sprites.FIGURE_HEIGHT == 24 and sprites.LEGS_TOP == 21
    for name, direction, step, rows in character_pictures():
        assert len(rows) == 24 and all(len(row) == 16 for row in rows), (name, direction, step)
        assert rows[-1].strip("."), (name, direction, step)  # something stands on the last row
        assert sum(1 for row in rows if row.strip(".")) >= 19, (name, direction, step)  # taller than a tile
    assert sprites.image("wizard_down_0").get_size() == (main.TILE, sprites.FIGURE_HEIGHT)


def test_only_the_legs_change_while_walking():
    for name, direction, step, rows in character_pictures():
        standing = sprites.SPRITES[f"{name}_{direction}_0"]
        if name == "flash" and direction == "right":
            continue  # its scarf flies: checked on its own
        assert rows[:sprites.LEGS_TOP] == standing[:sprites.LEGS_TOP], (name, direction, step)
    for name in ("wizard", "nurse", "ghost", "flash"):
        legs = {tuple(sprites.SPRITES[f"{name}_down_{step}"][sprites.LEGS_TOP:]) for step in range(3)}
        assert len(legs) == 3, name


def test_each_character_has_its_own_head_and_accessories():
    hat = sprites.SPRITES["wizard_down_0"]
    assert hat[0].strip(".") and "y" in letters(hat, 0, 4) and "P" in letters(hat, 0, 4)  # a tall hat with a star
    assert "w" in letters(hat, 13, 16)  # a white beard
    assert sprites.SPRITES["wizard_right_0"][1].index("P") < 8  # the tip of the hat bends back
    cap = sprites.SPRITES["nurse_down_0"]
    assert "r" in letters(cap, 3, 8) and "w" in letters(cap, 3, 8)  # a white cap with a red cross
    assert "r" in letters(cap, 16, 20)  # and another on her uniform
    assert "r" not in letters(sprites.SPRITES["nurse_up_0"], 0, 20)  # not on her back
    ghost = sprites.SPRITES["ghost_down_0"]
    assert letters(ghost, 0, 23) <= {"k", "w", "f"}  # white and pale blue, nothing else
    assert "j" not in letters(ghost, 21, 23) and "." in ghost[-1]  # a wavy hem, no legs
    flash = sprites.SPRITES["flash_down_0"]
    assert "y" in letters(flash, 8, 12) and flash[10][0] == "y" and flash[10][15] == "y"  # lightning at the ears
    assert "o" in letters(flash, 15, 16) and "y" in letters(flash, 17, 19)  # a scarf, a bolt on the chest
    for name in ("wizard", "nurse", "ghost", "flash"):
        front = sprites.SPRITES[f"{name}_down_0"]
        back = sprites.SPRITES[f"{name}_up_0"]
        assert front[11] != back[11] and "k" in front[11][3:13], name  # eyes in front, none at the back
    heads = {tuple(sprites.SPRITES[f"{name}_down_0"][:16]) for name in ("wizard", "nurse", "ghost", "flash")}
    assert len(heads) == 4


def test_the_scarf_of_flash_flies_behind_while_running():
    still, *running = (sprites.SPRITES[f"flash_right_{step}"] for step in range(3))
    assert "o" in letters(still, 16, 18) and still[16].index("o") < 5  # hanging behind
    for rows in running:
        assert rows[15].index("o") <= 1  # out behind, almost horizontal
    assert running[0][15:17] != running[1][15:17]  # and it flutters


================================================================================
# FILE: plot_twist/test_sounds.py
================================================================================

"""Tests for the sounds: the voices of an 8-bit sound chip made in code, their envelopes, sweeps and
filters, the effects and the loops, and a game that runs the same without audio."""

import math

import pygame
import pytest

import sounds

RATE = 8000


def plain(wave, freq, length=0.1, **more):
    """A voice at full volume all along: no attack, no release, so its samples are just the wave."""
    return sounds.layer(wave, freq, length, attack=0.0, release=0.0, **more)


def crossings(data):
    """How many times the sound goes from below zero to above it."""
    return sum(1 for index in range(1, len(data)) if data[index - 1] < 0 <= data[index])


def brightness(data):
    """How much the sound jumps from one sample to the next, on average: high for a hiss, low for a rumble."""
    return sum(abs(data[index] - data[index - 1]) for index in range(1, len(data))) / max(len(data) - 1, 1)


def loudness(data):
    return math.sqrt(sum(value * value for value in data) / max(len(data), 1))


# --- The voices of the chip ------------------------------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize("wave, duty", [("pulse12", 0.125), ("pulse25", 0.25), ("square", 0.5)])
def test_a_pulse_is_up_for_its_duty_of_each_period(wave, duty):
    data = sounds.voice_samples(plain(wave, 500), RATE)
    assert len(data) == 800
    assert set(data) == {1.0, -1.0}
    assert sum(1 for value in data if value > 0) / len(data) == pytest.approx(duty, abs=0.01)
    assert crossings(data) == pytest.approx(50, abs=1)  # 500 Hz for a tenth of a second


def test_the_triangle_has_sixteen_levels_and_is_symmetric():
    data = sounds.voice_samples(plain("triangle", 125, 0.096), RATE)  # twelve whole periods
    levels = sorted(set(data))
    assert len(levels) == 16 and levels[0] == -1.0 and levels[-1] == 1.0
    assert sum(data) == pytest.approx(0, abs=1)  # as much down as up
    assert sounds.TRIANGLE == list(range(15, -1, -1)) + list(range(16))  # it falls, then rises again


def test_the_register_of_the_noise_never_gets_stuck():
    register, seen = sounds.NOISE_SEED, set()
    for _ in range(2000):
        register = sounds.noise_bit(register)
        assert 0 < register < 2 ** 15
        seen.add(register)
    assert len(seen) == 2000  # no short loop


def test_the_short_noise_loops_quickly_and_rings_like_metal():
    register, seen = sounds.NOISE_SEED, []
    for _ in range(400):
        register = sounds.noise_bit(register, short=True)
        seen.append(register)
    assert len(set(seen)) < 100  # a loop of 93 steps or less: a pitch, not a hiss
    noise = sounds.voice_samples(plain("noise", 4000, 0.2), RATE)
    metal = sounds.voice_samples(plain("metal", 4000, 0.2), RATE)
    assert set(noise) == set(metal) == {1.0, -1.0}
    assert noise != metal


def test_noise_is_held_between_two_steps_and_lower_is_rougher():
    data = sounds.voice_samples(plain("noise", 1000), RATE)
    for start in range(7, 720, 8):
        assert len(set(data[start:start + 8])) == 1  # a new level 1000 times a second: every 8 samples
    low = sounds.voice_samples(plain("noise", 50, 0.2), RATE)
    high = sounds.voice_samples(plain("noise", 4000, 0.2), RATE)
    assert crossings(low) < 12 < 100 < crossings(high)
    assert data == sounds.voice_samples(plain("noise", 1000), RATE)  # the same noise every time


# --- Envelopes, sweeps, filters ----------------------------------------------------------------


def test_the_envelope_rises_falls_holds_and_dies_in_sixteen_steps():
    voice = sounds.layer("square", 100, 0.5, attack=0.1, decay=0.1, sustain=0.5, release=0.2)
    levels = [sounds.envelope(voice, step / 100) for step in range(80)]
    assert levels[0] == 0.0 and levels[10] == 1.0          # attack
    assert levels[20] == 0.5 and levels[45] == 0.5         # decay, then sustain
    assert 0 < levels[60] < 0.5 and levels[71] == 0.0      # release, to silence
    assert all(level * sounds.STEPS == int(level * sounds.STEPS) for level in levels)  # 16 steps
    assert levels[:11] == sorted(levels[:11]) and levels[50:] == sorted(levels[50:], reverse=True)


def test_a_struck_voice_dies_away_by_itself():
    voice = sounds.hit("square", 440, 0.2)
    assert sounds.envelope(voice, 0.002) == 1.0
    assert sounds.envelope(voice, 0.1) == pytest.approx(0.5, abs=1 / 16)
    assert sounds.envelope(voice, 0.199) == pytest.approx(1 / 16)  # its last step...
    assert sounds.envelope(voice, 0.2 + voice["release"]) == 0.0  # ...then silence


def test_the_pitch_glides_to_its_target_by_equal_steps():
    voice = plain("square", 200, 1.0, to=800)
    assert sounds.pitch(voice, 0) == pytest.approx(200)
    assert sounds.pitch(voice, 0.5) == pytest.approx(400)  # half the way: an octave of the two
    assert sounds.pitch(voice, 1.0) == pytest.approx(800)
    data = sounds.voice_samples(voice, RATE)
    assert crossings(data[:2000]) < crossings(data[-2000:])  # it rises
    quick = plain("square", 200, 1.0, to=800, sweep=0.1)
    assert sounds.pitch(quick, 0.5) == pytest.approx(800)  # there in a tenth of a second, then it stays


def test_vibrato_wavers_and_tremolo_beats():
    voice = plain("square", 400, 1.0, vibrato=(5, 1.0))
    pitches = [sounds.pitch(voice, step / 100) for step in range(100)]
    assert max(pitches) == pytest.approx(400 * 2 ** (1 / 12), rel=0.01)
    assert min(pitches) == pytest.approx(400 * 2 ** (-1 / 12), rel=0.01)
    beating = sounds.voice_samples(plain("square", 400, 1.0, tremolo=(4, 0.8)), RATE)
    parts = [loudness(beating[start:start + 400]) for start in range(0, 8000, 400)]
    assert max(parts) > 3 * min(parts)


def test_a_lowpass_muffles_and_a_highpass_thins():
    raw = sounds.voice_samples(plain("noise", 8000), RATE)
    muffled = sounds.voice_samples(plain("noise", 8000, lowpass=300), RATE)
    thin = sounds.voice_samples(plain("square", 50, highpass=2000), RATE)
    assert brightness(muffled) < brightness(raw) / 4
    assert loudness(thin) < 0.5 * loudness(sounds.voice_samples(plain("square", 50), RATE))
    opening = sounds.voice_samples(plain("noise", 8000, 1.0, lowpass=200, lowpass_to=3000), RATE)
    assert brightness(opening[:1000]) < brightness(opening[-1000:])  # the filter opens: a whoosh


def test_voices_are_mixed_at_their_start():
    effect = [sounds.hit("square", 500, 0.05), sounds.hit("square", 500, 0.05, start=0.2)]
    data = sounds.render(effect, RATE)
    assert len(data) == int(sounds.effect_length(effect) * RATE)
    assert loudness(data[1000:1500]) == 0  # the pause between them
    assert loudness(data[1600:1800]) > 0


def test_loud_mixes_bend_instead_of_cracking():
    data = sounds.pcm([0.0, 0.5, 4.0, -4.0, 40.0], channels=2, repeat=2)
    assert len(data) == 5 * 4 and data[0] == 0
    assert list(data[4:8]) == [data[4]] * 4  # held, and the same on both sides
    assert 0 < data[4] < data[8] < data[16] <= 32767 and data[12] == -data[8]


# --- The effects ----------------------------------------------------------------------------


def test_every_effect_is_short_and_heard():
    for name, voices in sounds.EFFECTS.items():
        assert voices, name
        assert 0 < sounds.effect_length(voices) < 1.5, name
        assert loudness(sounds.render(voices, RATE)) > 0.05, name


def test_the_effects_all_sound_different():
    rendered = {name: sounds.render(voices, RATE) for name, voices in sounds.EFFECTS.items()}
    pictures = {(len(data), round(loudness(data), 3), round(brightness(data), 3)) for data in rendered.values()}
    assert len(pictures) == len(rendered)


def test_a_puddle_splashes_where_wood_knocks():
    wood = sounds.render(sounds.EFFECTS["step_wood"], RATE)
    puddle = sounds.render(sounds.EFFECTS["step_puddle"], RATE)
    assert len(puddle) > 2 * len(wood)  # a splash lasts
    assert any(voice["lowpass_to"] for voice in sounds.EFFECTS["step_puddle"])  # the water closes over the foot
    floors = {name for name in sounds.EFFECTS if name.startswith("step_")}
    assert floors == {"step_wood", "step_stone", "step_tiles", "step_earth", "step_puddle"}
    stone = sounds.render(sounds.EFFECTS["step_stone"], RATE)
    earth = sounds.render(sounds.EFFECTS["step_earth"], RATE)
    assert brightness(stone) > brightness(earth)  # stone clicks, earth thuds


def test_a_locked_door_thuds_and_rattles():
    voices = sounds.EFFECTS["locked"]
    assert any(voice["wave"] == "triangle" and voice["to"] < voice["freq"] for voice in voices)  # the thud
    rattles = [voice for voice in voices if voice["wave"] == "metal"]
    assert len(rattles) >= 3 and len({voice["start"] for voice in rattles}) == len(rattles)


def test_the_claws_click_and_through_a_wall_they_are_muffled():
    near = sounds.render(sounds.EFFECTS["claw_click"], RATE)
    far = sounds.render(sounds.EFFECTS["claw_click_far"], RATE)
    assert sounds.effect_length(sounds.EFFECTS["claw_click"]) < 0.1
    assert brightness(far) < brightness(near) / 2
    assert all(voice["lowpass"] for voice in sounds.EFFECTS["claw_click_far"])


def test_the_dash_is_a_rush_of_air_that_opens():
    voices = sounds.EFFECTS["dash"]
    air = next(voice for voice in voices if voice["wave"] == "noise")
    assert air["lowpass_to"] > air["lowpass"] and air["attack"] > 0.01
    assert any(voice["to"] and voice["to"] > voice["freq"] for voice in voices)  # and a rising pitch


def test_the_statue_rumbles_and_the_glass_tinkles():
    crumble = sounds.render(sounds.EFFECTS["crumble"], RATE)
    shatter = sounds.render(sounds.EFFECTS["shatter"], RATE)
    assert len(crumble) > len(shatter) and brightness(shatter) > brightness(crumble)
    assert min(voice["to"] or voice["freq"] for voice in sounds.EFFECTS["crumble"] if voice["wave"] == "triangle") < 50
    tinkles = [voice for voice in sounds.EFFECTS["shatter"] if voice["wave"] == "pulse12"]
    assert len(tinkles) >= 8 and min(voice["freq"] for voice in tinkles) > 2000
    pebbles = [voice for voice in sounds.EFFECTS["crumble"] if voice["highpass"]]
    assert len({voice["start"] for voice in pebbles}) >= 6  # bouncing at different moments
    assert sounds.EFFECTS["crumble"] == sounds.EFFECTS["crumble"]  # and always the same ones


# --- The loops -----------------------------------------------------------------------------


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", list(sounds.LOOPS))
def test_a_loop_fades_into_its_own_start(name):
    length, voices = sounds.LOOPS[name]
    data = sounds.loop_samples(length, voices, RATE)
    assert len(data) == int(length * RATE)
    assert abs(data[-1] - data[0]) < 0.25  # no click at the seam
    assert loudness(data) > 0.02
    assert 0.9 < length <= 4.0


def test_the_sea_swells_and_inside_the_ship_it_is_muffled():
    length, voices = sounds.LOOPS["sea"]
    sea = sounds.loop_samples(length, voices, RATE)
    parts = [loudness(sea[start:start + 1600]) for start in range(0, len(sea) - 1600, 1600)]
    assert max(parts) > 1.5 * min(parts)  # waves
    far = sounds.loop_samples(*sounds.LOOPS["sea_far"][:1], sounds.LOOPS["sea_far"][1], RATE)
    assert brightness(far) < brightness(sea)


# --- Playing: left and right, loops that fade, no audio ---------------------------------------


def test_left_and_right_keep_the_same_loudness():
    assert sounds.sides(1.0, 0.0) == (pytest.approx(1.0), pytest.approx(1.0))
    assert sounds.sides(1.0, -1.0) == (pytest.approx(1.0), pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-9))
    assert sounds.sides(0.5, 1.0) == (pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-9), pytest.approx(0.5))
    left, right = sounds.sides(1.0, 0.5)
    assert left < right and left ** 2 + right ** 2 >= 1.0
    assert sounds.sides(3.0, 9.0) == sounds.sides(1.0, 1.0)  # out of range: kept in it


def test_without_audio_everything_is_silent(monkeypatch):
    """No mixer (no sound card): nothing is loaded, and playing anything does nothing and breaks nothing."""
    monkeypatch.setattr(pygame.mixer, "get_init", lambda: None)
    sounds.load()
    assert sounds._loaded == {}
    for name in list(sounds.EFFECTS) + [None, "no_such_sound"]:
        sounds.play(name, 0.5, -0.3)
    sounds.loop("bed", "sea", 0.5)
    sounds.update_loops()
    assert sounds.loop_volume("bed", "sea") > 0  # the fades are kept all the same: the game does not care
    sounds._loops.clear()


def test_a_new_loop_fades_in_as_the_old_one_fades_out(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(pygame.mixer, "get_init", lambda: None)
    sounds.load()
    sounds.loop("bed", "sea", 0.6)
    for _ in range(sounds.LOOP_FADE_FRAMES):
        sounds.update_loops()
    assert sounds.loop_volume("bed", "sea") == pytest.approx(0.6)
    sounds.loop("bed", "drips", 0.5)  # a new room
    for _ in range(sounds.LOOP_FADE_FRAMES // 2):
        sounds.update_loops()
    assert 0 < sounds.loop_volume("bed", "sea") < 0.6 and 0 < sounds.loop_volume("bed", "drips") < 0.5  # both, crossing
    for _ in range(sounds.LOOP_FADE_FRAMES):
        sounds.update_loops()
    assert sounds.loop_volume("bed", "sea") == 0 and [sound["name"] for sound in sounds._loops["bed"]] == ["drips"]
    sounds.loop("bed", None)
    for _ in range(sounds.LOOP_FADE_FRAMES):
        sounds.update_loops()
    assert sounds._loops["bed"] == []
    sounds._loops.clear()


def test_with_audio_every_sound_is_made_and_loops_have_channels_of_their_own(monkeypatch):
    """With a mixer (here SDL's silent "dummy" one), each effect and loop becomes a sound of its length."""
    monkeypatch.setenv("SDL_AUDIODRIVER", "dummy")
    pygame.mixer.init(frequency=22050, size=-16, channels=2)
    try:
        sounds.load()
        assert set(sounds._loaded) == set(sounds.EFFECTS) | set(sounds.LOOPS)
        for name, voices in sounds.EFFECTS.items():
            assert sounds._loaded[name].get_length() == pytest.approx(sounds.effect_length(voices), abs=0.003), name
        for name, (length, _) in sounds.LOOPS.items():
            assert sounds._loaded[name].get_length() == pytest.approx(length, abs=0.003), name
        assert len(sounds._free) == sounds.LOOP_CHANNELS
        sounds.play("ding", 0.5, 0.7)
        sounds.loop("bed", "sea", 0.5, -0.4)
        assert len(sounds._free) == sounds.LOOP_CHANNELS - 1
        channel = sounds._loops["bed"][0]["channel"]
        assert channel.get_busy() and sounds.loop_volume("bed", "sea") == 0.0  # it starts silent and fades in
        for _ in range(sounds.LOOP_FADE_FRAMES):
            sounds.update_loops()
        assert sounds.loop_volume("bed", "sea") == pytest.approx(0.5)
        sounds.loop("bed", None)
        for _ in range(sounds.LOOP_FADE_FRAMES):
            sounds.update_loops()
        assert not channel.get_busy() and len(sounds._free) == sounds.LOOP_CHANNELS  # stopped, and given back
    finally:
        sounds._loaded.clear()
        sounds._loops.clear()
        sounds._free.clear()
        pygame.mixer.quit()
